<div class='question question'>

Lisa has six number cards and four operation cards.

She has one only of each card.

$\huge \boxed{2} \ \boxed{3} \ \boxed{4} \ \boxed{5} \ \boxed{6} \ \boxed{7} \quad \boxed{+} \ \boxed{-} \ \boxed{\times} \ \boxed{\div}$

The cards can be placed side by side to form number statements, for example,

$\huge \boxed{3} \ \boxed{+} \ \boxed{4} \ = \ \boxed{7}$

$\huge \boxed{6} \ \boxed{\times} \ \boxed{7} \ = \ \boxed{4} \ \boxed{2}$

| prime |        |       |       |
